# Persona space against M3: disagreements, batch `persona_2026-10-15_all`

Each kind is ranked by its strength and cut to 805 rows (10% of the scored rows).  Thresholds: `dup_threshold` 0.73, `novel_pct` 0.80, cover-questioned cosine 0.61, novel-vs-covered percentile 0.90 (set by: plan default).  Persona space reads a *predicted* vector; M3 reads the text.  Neither decides here: M3's decision is unchanged.
